    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/DESIGN-SKETCH.md",
      "bytes": 3140,
      "sha256": "f5b0bbd94d38034f42ef5c2b09efd8dc9f44dd94251aa2087195503fde4dc251",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Candidate A design before implementation\n\n## Usage first\n\nThe existing import stays `mount` from `caller.mjs`.\n\n```js\nconst unmount = mount('view', ready => console.log(ready));\nawait unmount();\n\nconst oldUnmount = mount('shared', ready => oldReports.push(ready));\nconst newUnmount = mount('shared', ready => newReports.push(ready));\nawait oldUnmount();\nawait newUnmount();\n\nconst stop = mount('error', () => { throw new Error('report failed'); });\nawait stop().catch(error => errors.push(error.message));\n```\n\nCalling unmount removes only its own currently published entry before returning. The returned Promise observes deferred callback completion. Repeated calls return the same Promise and never schedule another callback. The callback reads its mount's retained value rather than consulting the replaceable current-key index. A report error rejects that Promise without restoring the cache entry.\n\n## Whole ownership design A, selected\n\nThe cache owns a private Map of entry records. `put(key, value)` returns a capability with the retained value and an identity-conditional `invalidate()` operation. `get(key)` reads the current entry's value. `invalidate(key)` remains synchronous and unconditional for an explicit current-key operation. The mount owns the retained capability and one completion Promise. Deferred work retains the value independent of index membership.\n\nSignatures in JavaScript terms.\n\n```js\nput(key, value) // -> { value, invalidate() -> boolean }\nget(key) // -> value | undefined\ninvalidate(key) // -> boolean\nmount(key, report) // -> unmount() -> Promise<void>\n```\n\nEach insertion has a fresh private entry identity, including reuse of the same value object. Only that entry's capability can conditionally remove it. The cache never exports the identity or a caller-supplied expected value.\n\n## Whole ownership design B, rejected\n\nMove lifecycle into a cache-managed resource registry. `cache.mount(key, value, report)` owns subscriber records, disposer creation, synchronous publication removal, deferred report scheduling, completion promises, and report error conversion. It returns one stop operation. The ordinary caller becomes a one-line wrapper or disappears.\n\nThis hides more scheduling work behind one method. It also couples a generic cache to this exact disposer and report policy. Supporting a second resource lifecycle changes the cache, even though the cache only needs replacement-safe removal. This design avoids split cleanup policy but loses the generic cache boundary. The small fixture does not justify a resource manager.\n\n## Red flag screen\n\nDesign A keeps Map mutation in cache.mjs and mount-specific disposal in caller.mjs. The capability hides compare-and-delete; callers cannot supply a mistaken value identity. No timer, reference count, lock, shared list, or lifecycle options are exposed. The extra Promise is an explicit completion/error contract. Design B has a smaller mount call but exposes lifecycle concepts through the generic cache and duplicates the role of caller.mjs. Both are sketches, not independently tested candidates. Parent owns final comparison and synthesis.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/tests/normal.mjs",
      "bytes": 484,
      "sha256": "76f84104396e1f6c56b2e25ca4477ac115130be85dba4ed70d5829d1088dfd30",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { mount } from '../proposed/caller.mjs';\nconst trace = [];\nconst stop = mount('normal', ready => trace.push(`reported ${ready}`));\nconst done = stop();\ntrace.push('returned');\nassert.deepEqual(trace, ['returned']);\nqueueMicrotask(() => trace.push('later microtask'));\nawait done;\nassert.deepEqual(trace, ['returned', 'reported true', 'later microtask']);\nconsole.log('normal teardown reports true once; trace:', JSON.stringify(trace));\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/tests/immediate.mjs",
      "bytes": 938,
      "sha256": "d60d89ad72252baf9fe2b45d9d673e24b3b80988f0b398f75170c7b696ae205b",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { mount } from '../proposed/caller.mjs';\nimport { get, put, invalidate } from '../proposed/cache.mjs';\nconst reports = [];\nconst stop = mount('current', ready => reports.push(ready));\nassert.deepEqual(get('current'), { ready: true });\nconst done = stop();\nassert.equal(get('current'), undefined);\nassert.deepEqual(reports, []);\nawait done;\nassert.deepEqual(reports, [true]);\nconst value = { ready: 'manual' };\nput('manual', value);\nassert.equal(get('manual'), value);\nassert.equal(invalidate('manual'), true);\nassert.equal(get('manual'), undefined);\nassert.equal(invalidate('manual'), false);\nconst external = mount('external', ready => reports.push(ready));\nassert.equal(invalidate('external'), true);\nawait external();\nassert.deepEqual(reports, [true, true]);\nconsole.log('immediate teardown and explicit invalidation remove current entry synchronously; retained callback reports true');\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/tests/replacement.mjs",
      "bytes": 1323,
      "sha256": "72bd2b64590aace8421ad9b3d6715c0790256e9843302f7928c8df54c4ccbe88",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { mount } from '../proposed/caller.mjs';\nimport { get, put } from '../proposed/cache.mjs';\nconst reports = [];\nconst oldStop = mount('same', ready => reports.push(['old', ready]));\nconst newStop = mount('same', ready => reports.push(['new', ready]));\nconst replacement = get('same');\nconst oldDone = oldStop();\nassert.equal(get('same'), replacement);\nawait oldDone;\nassert.equal(get('same'), replacement);\nassert.deepEqual(reports, [['old', true]]);\nawait newStop();\nassert.equal(get('same'), undefined);\nassert.deepEqual(reports, [['old', true], ['new', true]]);\nconst sameValue = { ready: true };\nconst first = put('reuse', sameValue);\nconst second = put('reuse', sameValue);\nassert.equal(first.invalidate(), false);\nassert.equal(get('reuse'), sameValue);\nassert.equal(second.invalidate(), true);\nassert.equal(second.invalidate(), false);\nconst lateStop = mount('late', ready => reports.push(['late', ready]));\nconst lateDone = lateStop();\nconst lateReplacement = { ready: 'replacement' };\nput('late', lateReplacement);\nawait lateDone;\nassert.equal(get('late'), lateReplacement);\nassert.deepEqual(reports, [['old', true], ['new', true], ['late', true]]);\nconsole.log('same-key replacement survives old teardown before and after scheduling; reused value identity is safe');\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/tests/repeated-errors.mjs",
      "bytes": 1089,
      "sha256": "f71e3aea5fb854423f9cfac729a09a33bc417acc17af35a99891fe818962251f",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nimport { mount } from '../proposed/caller.mjs';\nimport { get } from '../proposed/cache.mjs';\nconst reports = [];\nlet stop;\nlet reentrant;\nstop = mount('repeat', ready => {\n  reports.push(ready);\n  reentrant = stop();\n});\nconst first = stop();\nassert.equal(stop(), first);\nawait first;\nassert.equal(reentrant, first);\nassert.equal(stop(), first);\nawait stop();\nassert.deepEqual(reports, [true]);\nconst failure = new Error('report failed');\nlet failures = 0;\nconst fail = mount('failure', ready => {\n  assert.equal(ready, true);\n  failures++;\n  throw failure;\n});\nassert.deepEqual(get('failure'), { ready: true });\nconst rejected = fail();\nassert.equal(get('failure'), undefined);\nassert.equal(fail(), rejected);\nawait assert.rejects(rejected, error => error === failure);\nassert.equal(fail(), rejected);\nawait assert.rejects(fail(), error => error === failure);\nassert.equal(failures, 1);\nassert.deepEqual(reports, [true]);\nconsole.log('repeated and reentrant teardown share completion; callback failure rejects once and cleanup remains complete');\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/RATIONALE.md",
      "bytes": 6017,
      "sha256": "3d5e5de2e83b8f9a855dbb14fae80be1e5e7516fd2fe7dfe6eca75cce34d8971",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Candidate A ownership repair\n\n## Problem\n\nThe supplied after-state caller reads the current-key cache in a deferred disposal callback after synchronous deletion. The baseline exits 1 with a TypeError. Cache membership and a mount's value lifetime need separate ownership. The repaired caller reports its retained ready value and removes only its own insertion.\n\n## Usage (caller's view)\n\n```js\nconst stop = mount('panel', ready => reports.push(ready));\nconst done = stop();\n// get('panel') is already undefined here.\nawait done;\n\nconst oldStop = mount('same', reportOld);\nconst newStop = mount('same', reportNew);\nawait oldStop();\n// The current value still belongs to the newer mount.\nawait newStop();\n\nconst failingStop = mount('failure', () => { throw new Error('report failed'); });\nawait failingStop().catch(error => errors.push(error.message));\n```\n\nThe callback is synchronous and its return value is ignored. Awaiting teardown observes deferred completion or its synchronous callback exception. Ignoring a rejected completion can produce an unhandled rejection.\n\n## Shape\n\n`proposed/cache.mjs` stores private entry records in a Map. `put(key, value)` returns `{ value, invalidate() }`. `get(key)` still returns the current value. `invalidate(key)` still deletes the current entry synchronously. The returned capability compares its private entry identity before deletion. Reusing the identical value object across insertions stays safe.\n\n`proposed/caller.mjs` retains its own value and a lazily created completion Promise. Its stop function removes its owned insertion synchronously, then invokes the exact local disposer. Repeated and reentrant stops return the same Promise and schedule no additional callback. A thrown report value rejects that Promise once. Cache cleanup is independent of callback success.\n\nThe private insertion record encodes authority per Model the Domain and Foundational Thinking. Separate Before Serializing Shared State changed the callback to use per-mount state instead of the replaceable index. Make Operations Idempotent changed repeated stops to reuse completion. Fix Root Causes ruled out a missing-value guard. Boundary Discipline placed error conversion around the external report callback. Laziness Protocol and Minimize Reader Load kept two small modules and no resource-manager layer. JavaScript cannot enforce these shapes at compile time; private closure identity provides runtime authority.\n\nThe public cache addition is one returned capability. It hides replacement-safe deletion without exporting tokens or requiring callers to synchronize expected values. This is deeper than adding a public compare-and-delete primitive. The mount still owns report policy because the cache has no lifecycle knowledge.\n\n## Synthesis decision\n\nParent-owned and pending. This worker selects design A for its own candidate. No peer candidates, rubric, or judge artifacts were read. No integration decision is claimed.\n\n## Tradeoffs accepted\n\n- We accept retained value references in exchange for disposal reads independent of cache membership.\n- We accept a completion Promise in exchange for explicit callback error handling and repeatable completion observation.\n- We accept changing `put` from an ignored return to a capability return in exchange for replacement-safe cleanup. Existing callers that ignore the return still work in this fixture.\n- We accept a synchronous report callback contract. Async reports need a separately designed completion policy.\n\n## Alternatives considered\n\n`DESIGN-SKETCH.md` compares two whole ownership shapes before implementation. A cache-managed resource registry hides disposal behind one mount method, but couples cache storage to the pinned scheduler and report policy. It lost because independent cache callers would inherit lifecycle complexity. These local sketches are sequential, not independent design perspectives.\n\nRestoring the timer was run as the historical comparison, not selected as a design. It defers invalidation and leaves same-key deletion authority unresolved. Returning undefined for a missing ready value would hide the failure and change the report payload.\n\n## Open questions and risks\n\nShould a future API adopt asynchronous report callbacks? This candidate deliberately implements the stated synchronous callback policy. Does any caller outside the fixture inspect `put`'s return? No broader callers were supplied or inspected.\n\n## Next implementation step\n\nParent should inspect the source and receipts, compare completed candidates, and verify whichever synthesis it chooses.\n\n## Verification\n\nThe exact six assigned scenarios ran in order with Bun 1.4.2 and `--no-install`. The after-state probe exited 1. The timer before-state and all four proposed behavioral scenarios exited 0. An additional unchanged original probe against proposed source exited 0. Receipts preserve command, stdout, stderr, exit status, and order. `run-baselines.mjs` and `run-proposed.mjs` rerun the comparisons into new receipt directories without overwriting attempts. Build the Lever and Prove It Works produced these rerunnable files. Test Behavior, Not Implementation produced literal payload and cache-state assertions.\n\nThe failing output was `teardown error: TypeError: undefined is not an object (evaluating 'get(key).ready')`. The passing original probe printed `disposed ready: true` followed by `teardown completed`. These observations cover this vendored local fixture only.\n\n## Attention\n\nreviewed by unknown\n\nParent independent review is pending. Nested workers are forbidden. Companion gates for control-cli, create-skill, deslop, and no-comments were not run. The local runtime checks do not satisfy missing companion gates. No commits, installs, configurations, PRs, publications, external operations, or memory reads occurred. The visible current conversation supports a scoped self-audit; no separate persisted transcript was available, so a complete transcript audit and cross-model trail review remain unverified.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/run-baselines.mjs",
      "bytes": 1092,
      "sha256": "f6457e669419abb6a09191c764f30fda541b54f3415c6ad7ed6f75d3a67138e1",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nconst root = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst directory = join(root, 'receipts', `baselines-${Date.now()}`);\nawait mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });\nfor (const [index, state, expected] of [[1, 'after', 1], [2, 'before', 0]]) {\n  const command = ['bun', '--no-install', join(root, 'baselines', state, 'probe.mjs')];\n  const child = Bun.spawnSync(command, { cwd: root, stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe' });\n  const receipt = { order: index, command, cwd: root, exitStatus: child.exitCode, stdout: child.stdout.toString(), stderr: child.stderr.toString() };\n  await Bun.write(join(directory, `${index}-${state}.json`), JSON.stringify(receipt, null, 2));\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(receipt));\n  assert.equal(child.exitCode, expected);\n  assert.match(receipt.stdout, state === 'after' ? /teardown error: TypeError/ : /disposed ready: true\\nteardown completed/);\n}\nconsole.log(`Receipts saved to ${directory}`);\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/decisions.tsv",

      "text": "ts\tphase\tdecision\twhy\tevidence\tresult\n2026-10-16T19:00:52Z\tground\tConfirmed after-state failure and restored timer success\tThe caller reads after synchronous index deletion\treceipts/baselines-1792177121611\tafter exit 1; before exit 0\n2026-10-16T19:00:52Z\tdesign\tSelected retained mount value and insertion-owned invalidation\tSeparate value lifetime from current-key membership\tDESIGN-SKETCH.md\tlocal design selected; parent synthesis pending\n2026-10-16T19:00:52Z\tverify\tRan four ordered behavioral scenarios and unchanged original probe\tAssert payloads, replacement authority, repeat handling and callback errors\treceipts/proposed-1792177152404\tall proposed commands exit 0\n2026-10-16T19:00:52Z\tlimits\tKept independent review and companion gates unclaimed\tNested workers and external actions are forbidden\tRATIONALE.md\tcross-model trail review blocked; persisted transcript unavailable\n"

    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/HOW.md",
      "bytes": 4433,
      "sha256": "86d866e383785f0e7a7a6ef3215dfe9ea48d69edef8636229732735ef5bda938",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Local cache teardown model\n\n## Overview\n\nThe fixture publishes one value per key. Its caller mounts a ready value, subscribes a deferred report, and returns teardown. The local disposer queues exactly one microtask per call to `dispose`. Its implementation has no cancellation, idempotence, completion result, or error handler.\n\n## Key concepts\n\n`input/cache.mjs` owns the current-key Map. `input/caller.mjs` owns mount and unmount. `input/vendor/disposer-v1.mjs` owns only microtask scheduling. `input/probe.mjs` catches uncaught exceptions for its bounded observation and assigns a failure exit code. The pinned local vendor SHA-256 matched the manifest during baseline preparation.\n\n## How it works\n\nThe after-state mount writes `{ ready: true }` under its key. Unmount calls `subscription.dispose()`, which queues its callback and returns synchronously. `invalidate(key)` then removes the key in the same JavaScript turn. When the stack yields, the queued callback calls `get(key).ready`. The lookup returns undefined, so property access throws. Receipt `receipts/baselines-1792177121611/1-after.json` records that TypeError and exit 1.\n\nRestoring only the removed timer line schedules deletion as a timer task. The disposal microtask runs before that timer and finds the ready entry. Receipt `receipts/baselines-1792177121611/2-before.json` records `disposed ready: true`, `teardown completed`, and exit 0. This shows the changed deletion timing is sufficient to expose the local caller's late lookup. The diff alone omits that caller dependency. Restoring the timer changes immediate invalidation semantics and still lets an old keyed deletion remove a newer same-key entry.\n\n```text\nAfter-state current turn     dispose queues callback -> invalidate deletes key -> return\nAfter-state microtask        get(key) is undefined -> TypeError\nTimer before-state turn     dispose queues callback -> invalidate schedules timer -> return\nTimer before-state microtask get(key).ready is true -> report(true)\nTimer before-state timer     delete key\nProposed current turn        invalidate owned entry -> dispose queues callback -> return Promise\nProposed microtask           report retained ready value -> settle shared Promise\n```\n\nThe repair separates index membership from the mounted value's lifetime. The cache's private entry record identifies the insertion, and its capability performs compare-and-delete. The mount retains that insertion's value until deferred work runs. A replacement changes the current record but cannot change the old mount's retained value. Repeated teardown returns the existing Promise before any new deletion or disposal. The callback wrapper catches synchronous report failures and rejects that same Promise. Deletion has already completed.\n\n## Gotchas\n\nThe selected contract accepts a synchronous report callback. Its return value is ignored. A Promise returned by report is not adopted, so an asynchronous rejection is outside this contract. A caller that expects failures must await or catch unmount's Promise. The ready object remains mutable by reference, as in the supplied cache. Retention guarantees the owned object, not an immutable snapshot of arbitrary future object mutations.\n\n## Why and source coverage\n\nObserved history contains one fixture-introduction commit, e07d98d277a3c055b09f5b5736626ec9db21cbad. `git log --follow -p -- input/cache.mjs` introduces the after-state file. `git blame` attributes every supplied caller line to that commit. No PR number or author explanation of the former timer was present. Runtime ordering is observed; the original author's intent is unknown.\n\nSource-control history was searched locally. Issue tracker, long-form external documents, chat, infrastructure observability, exception tracking, and analytics were not searched. This assignment permits only the frozen package and local fixture and forbids external actions. Their absence is an evidence gap, not a searched-empty result. Nested investigators and synthesizers are forbidden, so this is a direct scoped explanation with reduced why coverage.\n\nPreserve synchronous current-key invalidation and the exact pinned disposer. Change deferred reads to use the mount's owned value. Avoid a timer workaround or an optional lookup that hides the missing report. Risk remains in unobserved dependency implementations and report callbacks that return promises. No claim here covers a public package.\n"

      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/prepare-baselines.mjs",
      "bytes": 1327,
      "sha256": "e507358a149add32d892f9a91a182125cdf2f925e1225346d04d11172443a9b3",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { mkdir, copyFile } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { join, dirname, resolve } from 'node:path';\nimport { createHash } from 'node:crypto';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nconst root = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst input = resolve(root, '../../input');\nconst vendor = await Bun.file(join(input, 'vendor/disposer-v1.mjs')).text();\nassert.equal(createHash('sha256').update(vendor).digest('hex'), 'd33e8d36600ad5df008657e478e39f9100a347098b4176f37923e5056cb3427f');\nfor (const state of ['after', 'before']) {\n  const dest = join(root, 'baselines', state);\n  await mkdir(join(dest, 'vendor'), { recursive: true });\n  for (const name of ['cache.mjs', 'caller.mjs', 'probe.mjs', 'vendor/disposer-v1.mjs']) {\n    await copyFile(join(input, name), join(dest, name));\n  }\n  if (state === 'before') {\n    const path = join(dest, 'cache.mjs');\n    const source = await Bun.file(path).text();\n    assert.equal(source.split('export function invalidate(key) { entries.delete(key); }').length, 2);\n    await Bun.write(path, source.replace('export function invalidate(key) { entries.delete(key); }', 'export function invalidate(key) { setTimeout(() => entries.delete(key), 0); }'));\n  }\n}\nconsole.log('Owned baseline copies created; pinned disposer SHA-256 matched.');\n"

      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Candidate A checklist\n\nLocal Markdown is the reduced todo binding. Parent owns Arena orchestration and synthesis. Nested workers are forbidden.\n\n## Bug fix steps\n\n1. Reproduce it yourself on the matching surface via the control skill in the mode's Non-negotiables, even when a debug or instrumentation protocol says to ask the user to reproduce. Ask the user only with a stated, specific reason the control surface cannot reach the target, and only after driving it as far as it goes. If it won't reproduce directly, synthesize the trigger, tighten conditions, or instrument until it fires. Record the exact symptom, trigger, surface, revision, command or action, exit status where available, and output before changing production code. Missing control companions or denied execution leave the original reproduction unverified. A proxy check cannot satisfy the matching-surface gate.\n2. Binary-search the cause. Form candidate hypotheses, then rule them out until one survives. Seed them with how over the affected subsystem and why for regression history, using their complete prompts and source contracts. Each pass takes the split that cuts the most remaining problem space, gets runtime evidence and eliminates candidates. When program state is unclear, add authorized instrumentation or logging and read it as the code runs. Don't guess. Drive a long or stubborn hunt with an exposed, authorized repeated-execution operation. If unavailable, perform bounded in-session iterations and disclose reduced capability, without claiming a background loop. Confirm the surviving mechanism with runtime evidence before the step-3 architect/interrogate fan-out.\n3. Plan the fix. If it crosses a function boundary, architect first, using its complete exploration, runner, rationale and screening contracts. Contested design follows the mode's interrogate gate. Delegate implementation to a subagent using the configured bug-fix role with a specific scope. The portable default is inherited model and effort. The canonical default grok-4.7-xhigh-fast is historical provenance, not current availability. Select a configured model only when supported by exposed tools and observed; retain rejected selections and disclosed fallbacks. Unknown actual models prove no diversity. No required independent operation means that gate remains blocked.\n4. Verify on the same surface. The original repro now passes. Retain the same trigger and conditions and record the actual output after the bounded root-cause fix. Inconclusive or wrong-surface is not a pass. Flag it. Unit tests show branch behavior, not bug absence. Keep denied controls, missing companions and unrun checks explicit.\n5. Stage the commits so the failing repro lands before the fix in git history. Skip adding that test when it would be expensive, integration-heavy or unclear, and state the reason and closest executable regression check. Commits, rebases and history changes require actual authorization and available operations. Without them, preserve the failing-before/passing-after artifacts and ordered delivery plan; do not claim that sequence landed.\n6. Run Opening a PR in full when its dependency and required capabilities are available and authorized. Missing playbook or companion gates stop dependent delivery. Return checked local artifacts and the exact unperformed actions otherwise. PR creation and other external writes require explicit authorization. Opening a PR does not implicitly start Babysit.\n\n## Architect phases\n\n- [x] Ground.\n- [x] Sketch.\n- [x] Agree. skip: Parent owns synthesis. Local implementation is authorized.\n- [x] Implement.\n- [ ] Scrap. skip: Only needed if implementation invalidates chosen shape.\n\n## Throughput checkpoint\n\n- Blocking first steps. Read task, every input, frozen mode, required contracts and applicable leaves before implementation. Baseline after-state then timer before-state must run first.\n- Independent workstreams. n/a. One candidate owns this code-coupled local fixture. Parent runs the independent competition.\n- Shared mutable state. Only candidate-a is writable. Runtime key ownership uses private entry identity and per-mount retained value.\n- Smallest safe decomposition. One worker traces caller/cache/disposer, implements one design, and runs six ordered scenarios. No nested controller or independent-review claim.\n\n## Task progress\n\n- [x] Read every input file and required frozen contracts.\n- [x] Observe exact input revision.\n- [x] Compare whole ownership sketches before implementation.\n- [x] Run after-state baseline then restored timer baseline.\n- [x] Implement selected source and behavioral scenarios.\n- [x] Run normal, invalidation, replacement, repeated/error scenarios in order.\n- [x] Preserve receipts and write final rationale and RESULT.JSON.\n\n## Gates\n\ncontrol-cli, create-skill, and deslop companions are not exposed for this task. The no-comments review was not run because nested reviewer dispatch is forbidden. Runtime Bun checks are a reduced local control binding, not a control-cli companion pass. Nested how/why/architect workers are forbidden. Local traced explanation and sequential whole-shape sketches satisfy this candidate's authorized work, not independent nested review. Parent owns independent judgment. Commits and external delivery are forbidden.\n"

      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';\nimport { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';\nimport { mkdir } from 'node:fs/promises';\nimport assert from 'node:assert/strict';\nconst root = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));\nconst directory = join(root, 'receipts', `proposed-${Date.now()}`);\nawait mkdir(directory, { recursive: true });\nfor (const [offset, name] of ['normal', 'immediate', 'replacement', 'repeated-errors'].entries()) {\n  const command = ['bun', '--no-install', join(root, 'tests', `${name}.mjs`)];\n  const child = Bun.spawnSync(command, { cwd: root, stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe' });\n  const receipt = { order: offset + 3, command, cwd: root, exitStatus: child.exitCode, stdout: child.stdout.toString(), stderr: child.stderr.toString() };\n  await Bun.write(join(directory, `${offset + 3}-${name}.json`), JSON.stringify(receipt, null, 2));\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(receipt));\n  assert.equal(child.exitCode, 0);\n}\nconst command = ['bun', '--no-install', join(root, 'proposed/probe.mjs')];\nconst child = Bun.spawnSync(command, { cwd: root, stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe' });\nconst receipt = { order: 7, purpose: 'unchanged supplied trigger against proposed implementation', command, cwd: root, exitStatus: child.exitCode, stdout: child.stdout.toString(), stderr: child.stderr.toString() };\nawait Bun.write(join(directory, '7-original-probe.json'), JSON.stringify(receipt, null, 2));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(receipt));\nassert.equal(child.exitCode, 0);\nassert.equal(receipt.stdout, 'disposed ready: true\\nteardown completed\\n');\nconsole.log(`Receipts saved to ${directory}`);\n"

before exit 0'],\n  ['design', 'Selected retained mount value and insertion-owned invalidation', 'Separate value lifetime from current-key membership', 'DESIGN-SKETCH.md', 'local design selected; parent synthesis pending'],\n  ['verify', 'Ran four ordered behavioral scenarios and unchanged original probe', 'Assert payloads, replacement authority, repeat handling and callback errors', 'receipts/proposed-1792177152404', 'all proposed commands exit 0'],\n  ['limits', 'Kept independent review and companion gates unclaimed', 'Nested workers and external actions are forbidden', 'RATIONALE.md', 'cross-model trail review blocked; persisted transcript unavailable'],\n];\nfor (const row of rows) {\n  const child = Bun.spawnSync(['bash', join(packageRoot, 'skills/show-me-your-work/scripts/log.sh'), join(root, 'decisions.tsv'), ...row], { cwd: root });\n  assert.equal(child.exitCode, 0);\n}\nconsole.log(await Bun.file(join(root, 'decisions.tsv')).text());\nlet checklist = await Bun.file(join(root, 'CHECKLIST.md')).text();\nchecklist = checklist.replace('- [ ] Implement.', '- [x] Implement.');\nchecklist = checklist.replaceAll('- [ ] Run ', '- [x] Run ').replace('- [ ] Implement selected', '- [x] Implement selected').replace('- [ ] Preserve receipts', '- [x] Preserve receipts');\nchecklist = checklist.replace('control-cli, create-skill, deslop and no-comments companions are absent from the exposed skills for this task.', 'control-cli, create-skill, and deslop companions are not exposed for this task. The no-comments review was not run because nested reviewer dispatch is forbidden.');\nawait Bun.write(join(root, 'CHECKLIST.md'), checklist);\nasync function walk(path) {\n  const result = [];\n  for (const entry of await readdir(path, { withFileTypes: true })) {\n    const full = join(path, entry.name);\n    if (entry.isDirectory()) result.push(...await walk(full));\n    else if (entry.name !== 'RESULT.JSON') result.push(full);\n  }\n  return result.sort();\n}\nconst paths = await walk(root);\nconst artifacts = [];\nfor (const path of paths) {\n  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await Bun.file(path).arrayBuffer());\n  artifacts.push({ path: relative(root, path), sha256: createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex'), bytes: bytes.length });\n}\nconst executionReceipts = [];\nfor (const artifact of artifacts.filter(item => item.path.startsWith('receipts/') && item.path.endsWith('.json'))) {\n  executionReceipts.push({ artifact: artifact.path, ...await Bun.file(join(root, artifact.path)).json() });\n}\nexecutionReceipts.sort((a, b) => a.order - b.order);\nassert.deepEqual(executionReceipts.map(item => item.exitStatus), [1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0]);\nconst vendor = artifacts.find(item => item.path === 'proposed/vendor/disposer-v1.mjs');\nassert.equal(vendor.sha256, 'd33e8d36600ad5df008657e478e39f9100a347098b4176f37923e5056cb3427f');\nconst result = {\n  assignmentId: 'candidate-a', workerId: '/root/candidate_a', outputOwner: '/root/candidate_a', inputRevision,\n  role: 'candidate', retryCount: 0, terminalStatus: 'completed',\n  method: {\n    sampleCount: 6,\n    sampleDefinition: 'Six observable scenarios run with Bun from actual files: supplied after-state probe; restored timer before-state probe; proposed normal teardown reports true; immediate invalidation removes current entry; same-key replacement survives old teardown; repeated teardown and callback failure policy are asserted.',\n    order: 'Run baseline after-state then before-state, then normal teardown, immediate invalidation, same-key replacement, and repeated teardown with callback failure assertions. Preserve stdout stderr exit status for every command.',\n  },\n  requestedModel: 'inherit-parent', actualModel: 'unknown', requestedEffort: 'inherit-parent', actualEffort: 'unknown',\n  runtime: 'Bun 1.4.2',\n  artifacts, rationale: 'RATIONALE.md', tracedModel: 'HOW.md', designAlternatives: 'DESIGN-SKETCH.md', executionReceipts,\n  observedOutcome: 'Exact local after-state reproduction failed as expected; timer before-state and all proposed checks passed. Synchronous removal, insertion identity, retained report value, shared completion and error rejection are asserted.',\n  limitations: [\n    'Exact local vendored fixture only. No public dependency behavior or production compatibility claim.',\n    'Parent owns Arena synthesis and fresh independent review. No rubric, peer artifacts or judge outputs read.',\n    'No nested workers permitted. Local how/why exploration and whole-shape sketches are direct work, not independent nested design or review.',\n    'Model and effort not observed. Requested inheritance does not prove model diversity.',\n    'Report callback must be synchronous. Its returned Promise is ignored. Callers must handle teardown rejection when report can throw.',\n    'Retained object is not an immutable snapshot. Arbitrary value mutation remains visible through retained references.',\n    'control-cli, create-skill and deslop companion gates are unavailable. no-comments reviewer not run. Local Bun checks are reduced control evidence.',\n    'Persisted transcript audit and cross-model decision-trail review remain unverified. The local decision log was written and read back.',\n    'Original author intent unknown. Git history shows fixture introduction only. No external rationale sources were accessed.',\n    'No installs, configurations, commits, external actions or private memory reads. Only assigned directory written.',\n  ],\n};\nawait Bun.write(join(root, 'RESULT.JSON'), JSON.stringify(result, null, 2) + '\\n');\nconst readback = await Bun.file(join(root, 'RESULT.JSON')).json();\nassert.equal(readback.workerId, '/root/candidate_a');\nassert.equal(readback.artifacts.length, artifacts.length);\nconsole.log(`RESULT.JSON saved and read back with ${artifacts.length} artifacts and ${executionReceipts.length} execution receipts.`);\n"

    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/proposed/caller.mjs",
      "bytes": 639,
      "sha256": "8af7f289cefc37ae1b6dd05e8edca8b4658ab0e14170827a1ad0f7963b1dcd41",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { createDisposer } from './vendor/disposer-v1.mjs';\nimport { put } from './cache.mjs';\n\nexport function mount(key, report) {\n  const owned = put(key, { ready: true });\n  let completion;\n  let resolve;\n  let reject;\n  const subscription = createDisposer(() => {\n    try {\n      report(owned.value.ready);\n      resolve();\n    } catch (error) {\n      reject(error);\n    }\n  });\n  return () => {\n    if (completion) return completion;\n    completion = new Promise((onSuccess, onFailure) => {\n      resolve = onSuccess;\n      reject = onFailure;\n    });\n    owned.invalidate();\n    subscription.dispose();\n    return completion;\n  };\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/candidate-a/proposed/cache.mjs",
      "bytes": 403,
      "sha256": "0516d040dae6b7434e74c1801f15a3a7e849511ab1095163687a64d51717733d",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "const entries = new Map();\n\nexport function put(key, value) {\n  const entry = { value };\n  entries.set(key, entry);\n  return Object.freeze({\n    value,\n    invalidate() {\n      if (entries.get(key) !== entry) return false;\n      return entries.delete(key);\n    },\n  });\n}\n\nexport function get(key) {\n  return entries.get(key)?.value;\n}\n\nexport function invalidate(key) {\n  return entries.delete(key);\n}\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/rubric.md",
      "bytes": 1177,

      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Parent-only rubric\n\nScore each criterion 0 to 4, where 0 lacks evidence, 1 is materially flawed, 2 has a usable approach with important gaps, 3 satisfies the criterion with minor limitations, and 4 has direct thorough evidence.\n\n1. Ordering diagnosis. Explains actual caller and pinned queueMicrotask behavior; observed failing after-state and successful timer before-state, without generalizing to public dependency.\n2. Ownership and lifecycle correctness. Normal teardown reports true; synchronous invalidation remains meaningful; same-key replacement and repeated teardown cannot corrupt current ownership; failure policy explicit.\n3. Caller clarity and extension cost. Small coherent API, explicit resource lifetime, understandable usage and low maintenance burden; compare whole designs.\n4. Behavioral evidence. Actual Bun source/tests exercise assertions including replacement and callback failure; receipts identify commands, exits and outputs; no mocks substituting pinned dependency.\n5. Claim and scope discipline. Grounded rationale distinguishes verified behavior from limits, preserves vendor bytes, names rejected alternatives and avoids hidden timer contracts.\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/prepare-judge.mjs",
      "bytes": 3995,
      "sha256": "cf50479424607ee472d7421d8bca16ed4e4653d7757aa2133bc3b532ec413e34",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {readFileSync,writeFileSync,appendFileSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {bind,event} from './ledger.mjs';\nconst source='/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3';\nconst method={sampleCount:10,sampleDefinition:'Score two completed labelled candidates against each of the five parent rubric criteria. Inspect actual source, tests, rationale, result metadata and execution receipts for all ten candidate-criterion judgments.',order:'Read frozen role and mode requirements, rubric and input; inspect candidate A end to end then candidate B end to end; assign criterion scores with evidence and rationale; compare and recommend maintainable base with explicit grafts and rejections.'};\nwriteFileSync('outputs/judge-method.json',JSON.stringify(method,null,2));\nwriteFileSync('outputs/judge-task.md',`# Fresh read-only cross-judge task\\n\\nAssignment judge, canonical worker /root/judge, inputRevision e07d98d277a3c055b09f5b5736626ec9db21cbad. Requested model and effort inherit-parent. Actual unknown unless observed.\\n\\nFIRST create your own checklist ONLY at outputs/judge/CHECKLIST.md before task reads/work. Otherwise remain read-only. No writes to candidate artifacts or other paths. Return your exact RESULT.JSON object as the ENTIRE final response, without markdown fence or commentary, for parent capture at outputs/judge/RESULT.JSON. No source modifications, installs, configs, commits, external actions, memory, other versions or nested workers. Parent owns arena orchestration.\\n\\nRead FULL mode ${source}/skills/poteto-mode/SKILL.md with inline Principles index and applicable leaves, full frozen README and actual linked docs/workflows/arena.md, skills/arena/SKILL.md, references/host-capabilities.md, model-roles.md, workflow-ownership.md and orchestration-contract.md. Full role prompt follows. Read frozen unslop, technical-writing and applicable principle leaves. Follow complete role within readonly review scope. No no-comments or cross-model companion claim without actual operation.\\n\\nRead all ./input files end to end. Both candidates have stopped writing and parent inspected and reran their actual source/tests. Read outputs/rubric.md then both completed labelled paths end to end, including their RESULT.JSON, implementation source, tests, rationale, alternatives, receipts and failures. A = outputs/candidate-a; B = outputs/candidate-b. Parent execution receipts = outputs/candidate-a-parent-execution.json and outputs/candidate-b-parent-execution.json. Candidate resultRevision is actual inputRevision echoed in each RESULT.JSON. Candidate method is exact ${JSON.stringify(JSON.parse(readFileSync('outputs/method.json')))}.\\n\\nScore each of five rubric criteria 0 to 4 for each candidate with concrete rationale and artifact evidence. Recommend a maintainable base, useful coherent grafts, rejected alternatives and limits. Inspect callback errors, same-key replacement including identical value reuse, repeats and API cost. Do not grade this workflow or claim host pass. Your scope is candidate selection.\\n\\nYour exact independently echoed method must be ${JSON.stringify(method)}. RESULT.JSON must include assignmentId:'judge', workerId:'/root/judge', inputRevision, method, requestedModel:'inherit-parent', actualModel:'unknown' unless observed, terminalStatus:'completed' if complete, scores array, recommendedBase, rationale, inspectedArtifacts, limitations. Do not include a self hash. Your final exact JSON bytes will be captured by parent as allowed.\\n\\n# Full role prompt\\n\\n${readFileSync('worker-role.md','utf8')}\\n`);\nbind('judge','/root/judge','judge','judge','outputs/judge-task.md',method);\nevent('judge-dispatch-declared',{assignmentId:'judge'});\nappendFileSync('outputs/native-worker-captures.jsonl',JSON.stringify({tool:'collaboration.spawn_agent',request:{task_name:'judge',fork_turns:'none',mode:'BOOTSTRAP-ONLY'},result:{task_name:'/root/judge'}})+'\\n');\n"

    {

      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {readFileSync,writeFileSync,readdirSync,lstatSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {resolve,relative} from 'node:path';\nimport {createHash} from 'node:crypto';\nimport {event} from './ledger.mjs';\nconst [id,dir,workerId]=process.argv.slice(2);\nconst root=resolve('outputs',dir), resultPath=relative(process.cwd(),root+'/RESULT.JSON');\nconst result=JSON.parse(readFileSync(resultPath,'utf8'));\nconst files=[];\nfunction walk(path){for(const name of readdirSync(path)){const p=path+'/'+name,s=lstatSync(p);if(s.isSymbolicLink())throw Error('symlink requires inspection '+p);if(s.isDirectory())walk(p);else {const bytes=readFileSync(p);files.push({path:relative(process.cwd(),p),bytes:bytes.length,sha256:createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex')});}}}walk(root);\nconst expected=JSON.parse(readFileSync('outputs/method.json','utf8'));\nconst metadata={identityMatches:result.assignmentId===id&&result.workerId===workerId,revisionMatches:result.inputRevision==='e07d98d277a3c055b09f5b5736626ec9db21cbad',methodMatches:JSON.stringify(result.method)===JSON.stringify(expected),allPathsInsideOwnedDirectory:files.every(f=>resolve(f.path).startsWith(root+'/')),files};\nwriteFileSync('outputs/'+id+'-inspection.json',JSON.stringify(metadata,null,2));\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify(metadata,null,2));\nconsole.log('RESULT.JSON contents\\n'+readFileSync(resultPath,'utf8'));\nevent('candidate-terminal-observed',{assignmentId:id,workerId,status:'completed',artifact:resultPath});\n"

      "text": "import {mkdirSync,readFileSync,writeFileSync} from 'node:fs';\nconst target='outputs/before-state';mkdirSync(target+'/vendor',{recursive:true});\nfor (const path of ['cache.mjs','caller.mjs','probe.mjs','vendor/disposer-v1.mjs']) {let text=readFileSync('input/'+path,'utf8');if(path==='cache.mjs')text=text.replace('entries.delete(key);','setTimeout(() => entries.delete(key), 0);');writeFileSync(target+'/'+path,text);}\nconst p=Bun.spawnSync(['bun','--no-install',target+'/probe.mjs']);\nwriteFileSync('outputs/parent-before-state.json',JSON.stringify({command:['bun','--no-install',target+'/probe.mjs'],exitCode:p.exitCode,stdout:p.stdout.toString(),stderr:p.stderr.toString(),modification:'Restore only removed setTimeout line from invalidation.diff in owned copy.'},null,2));\nconsole.log(readFileSync('outputs/parent-before-state.json','utf8'));\n"

    {
      "path": "strict-method-repairs/candidate-cross-review/outputs/check-receipt.mjs",
      "bytes": 4910,
      "sha256": "a0b4e1078b24b79b654b91d81ccec67feb0e2f0071f2dd4133781bb9be6e2166",
      "owner": "local-013-strict-candidate-cross-review",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {readFileSync,existsSync} from 'node:fs';\nimport {createHash} from 'node:crypto';\nimport {resolve,dirname,relative} from 'node:path';\nconst receipt=JSON.parse(readFileSync('outputs/method-receipt.json','utf8')), operations=JSON.parse(readFileSync('outputs/successful-operations.json','utf8'));\nconst errors=[];\nfunction require(condition,detail){if(!condition)errors.push(detail);}\nfunction keys(obj,list){return JSON.stringify(Object.keys(obj).sort())===JSON.stringify(list.sort());}\nfunction inspected(path){return typeof path==='string'&&existsSync(path);}\nrequire(keys(receipt,['schemaVersion','kind','inputRevision','assignments','reviewArtifact','reviewedBy','declaredOrder','candidates','judge','rubricArtifact','synthesis']),'receipt keys');\nrequire(receipt.schemaVersion===1&&receipt.kind==='arena','identity');\nrequire(receipt.inputRevision==='e07d98d277a3c055b09f5b5736626ec9db21cbad','input SHA');\nrequire(inspected(receipt.reviewArtifact)&&receipt.reviewedBy.trim(),'review artifact');\nconst ids=new Set(),workers=new Set(),parents=[];\nfor(const a of receipt.assignments){\n require(keys(a,['id','workerId','inputRevision','outputOwner','outputPath','role','status','startedOrder','completedOrder','requestedModel','actualModel','retryCount','method','taskArtifact','rubricProvided','resultRevision','resultMethod','outputSha256']),'assignment keys '+a.id);\n require(!ids.has(a.id)&&!workers.has(a.workerId),'distinct identity '+a.id);ids.add(a.id);workers.add(a.workerId);\n require(a.inputRevision===receipt.inputRevision&&a.resultRevision===a.inputRevision,'result revision '+a.id);\n require(a.outputOwner===a.workerId,'owner '+a.id);\n require(JSON.stringify(a.resultMethod)===JSON.stringify(a.method),'result method '+a.id);\n const actual=JSON.parse(readFileSync(a.outputPath,'utf8'));\n require(actual.inputRevision===a.resultRevision&&JSON.stringify(actual.method)===JSON.stringify(a.resultMethod),'actual worker echo '+a.id);\n require(createHash('sha256').update(readFileSync(a.outputPath)).digest('hex')===a.outputSha256,'final bytes '+a.id);\n const parent=dirname(resolve(a.outputPath));\n require(parents.every(p=>!parent.startsWith(p+'/')&&!p.startsWith(parent+'/')&&parent!==p),'disjoint parent directories '+a.id);parents.push(parent);\n require(['startedOrder','completedOrder','retryCount'].every(k=>Number.isInteger(a[k]))&&Number.isInteger(receipt.declaredOrder),'integer orders '+a.id);\n require(receipt.declaredOrder<a.startedOrder&&a.startedOrder<a.completedOrder&&[0,1].includes(a.retryCount),'phase order '+a.id);\n require(['completed','cancelled','blocked','failed'].includes(a.status),'terminal status '+a.id);\n require(['id','workerId','role','requestedModel','actualModel'].every(k=>typeof a[k]==='string'&&a[k].trim()),'strings '+a.id);\n require(inspected(a.taskArtifact),'task artifact '+a.id);\n const m=a.method;\n require(keys(m,['sampleCount','sampleDefinition','order'])&&Number.isInteger(m.sampleCount)&&m.sampleCount>0&&typeof m.sampleDefinition==='string'&&m.sampleDefinition.trim()&&typeof m.order==='string'&&m.order.trim(),'method shape '+a.id);\n for(const operation of ['spawn','inspect-result',...(a.role==='judge'?[]:['isolated-write'])])require(operations.some(o=>o.assignmentId===a.id&&o.operation===operation&&o.evidence===a.outputPath),'bound '+operation+' '+a.id);\n}\nconst candidates=receipt.candidates.map(id=>receipt.assignments.find(a=>a.id===id)),judge=receipt.assignments.find(a=>a.id===receipt.judge);\nrequire(candidates.length>=2&&new Set(receipt.candidates).size===candidates.length,'two survivors');\nrequire(judge.role==='judge'&&judge.status==='completed'&&judge.rubricProvided===true&&!receipt.candidates.includes(judge.id),'fresh judge');\nrequire(candidates.every(a=>a.role==='candidate'&&a.status==='completed'&&a.rubricProvided===false&&a.completedOrder<judge.startedOrder),'candidate terminal before judge');\nrequire(new Set(candidates.map(a=>a.taskArtifact)).size===1,'shared task');\nrequire(inspected(receipt.rubricArtifact),'rubric');\nconst s=receipt.synthesis;\nrequire(keys(s,['order','inspectedCandidates','artifact','verificationArtifact']),'synthesis keys');\nrequire(JSON.stringify([...s.inspectedCandidates].sort())===JSON.stringify([...receipt.candidates].sort()),'all candidates inspected');\nrequire(Number.isInteger(s.order)&&s.order>judge.completedOrder&&inspected(s.artifact)&&inspected(s.verificationArtifact),'synthesis order and artifacts');\nrequire(operations.some(o=>o.operation==='inspect-method'&&o.evidence==='outputs/method-receipt.json'),'method inspection');\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({kind:'structural-relationship-diagnostics',reference:'strict-schema-reference.txt',exactReferenceExecuted:false,errors,limitation:'Local Bun checks mirror the supplied excerpt fields and relationships. They do not validate qualitative judgments or assign a host/workflow grade.'},null,2));\nif(errors.length)process.exit(1);\n"
    },
